**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_mosaic_dataset", "label_zh": "輸入鑲嵌資料集", "label_en": "Input Mosaic Dataset", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Mosaic Layer; Mosaic Dataset", "description_en": "The input mosaic dataset that will be adjusted."}, {"parameter": "in_control_points", "label_zh": "輸入控制點", "label_en": "Input Control Points", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The control point table that includes tie points and ground control points. This feature class is usually the output from the Compute Tie Points tool."}, {"parameter": "transformation_type", "label_zh": "變換類型", "label_en": "Transformation Type", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the type of transformation that will be used when adjusting the mosaic dataset. Zero-order polynomial—A zero-order polynomial will be used in the block adjustment computation. This is commonly used when the data is in flat area. First-order polynomial—A first-order polynomial (affine) will be used in the block adjustment computation. This is the default. Rational Polynomial Coefficients—The rational polynomial coefficients (RPCs) will be used for the transformation. This is used for satellite imagery that contains RPC information in the metadata. This option requires the ArcGIS Desktop Advanced license. Frame camera model—The Frame camera model will be used for the transformation. This is used for aerial imagery that contains the frame camera information in the metadata. This option requires the ArcGIS Desktop Advanced license."}, {"parameter": "out_solution_table", "label_zh": "輸出解決方案表", "label_en": "Output Solution Table", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Table", "description_en": "The output solution table containing the adjustments."}, {"parameter": "out_solution_point_table", "label_zh": "輸出解決方案點 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Solution Points (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "The output solution points table. This will be saved as a polygon feature class. This output can be quite large."}, {"parameter": "maximum_residual_value", "label_zh": "最大殘差 （可選）", "label_en": "Maximum Residual (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "A threshold that is used in block adjustment computation; points with residuals exceeding the threshold will not be used. This parameter applies when the transformation type is Zero-order polynomial, First-order polynomial, or Frame camera model. If the transformation type is Rational Polynomial Coefficients, the proper threshold for eliminating invalid points will be automatically determined. When the transformation type is Zero-order polynomial or First-order polynomial, the units for this parameter will be map units, and the default value will be 2. When the transformation type is Frame camera model, the units for this parameter will be pixels, and the default value will be 5."}, {"parameter": "adjustment_options", "label_zh": "校正選項 （可選）", "label_en": "Adjustment Options (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Additional options that will be used to fine-tune the adjustment computation. Note: To set an option in the Geoprocessing pane, type the keyword and the corresponding value in the list box. MinResidual—The minimum residual value, which is the lower threshold value, will be used. When the transformation type is POLYORDER0 or POLYORDER1, the units will be map units and the default minimum residual value will be 0. The minimum residual value and the maximum_residual_value parameter value are used in detecting and removing points that generate large errors from the block adjustment computation. MaxResidualFactor—The maximum residual factor will be used to generate the maximum (upper threshold) residual value if the maximum_residual-value parameter is not defined. In this case, MaxResidualFactor * RMS will be used to calculate the upper threshold value. The minimum residual value and the maximum_residual_factor parameter value are used in detecting and removing points that generate large errors from block adjustment computation. Additional options for the adjustment engine are listed below when Frame is specified for the Transformation Type parameter. The specifications of many of the options are supplied by the data provider. The options include the following: CalibrateF—Calibrate the sensor's focal length for use in the block adjustment. Assign a value of 1 for focal length calibration or 0 for no calibration. The default is 0. CalibratePP—Calibrate the principle point in the block adjustment. Assign a value of 1 for calibration or 0 for no calibration. The default is 0. CalibrateP—Calibrate for radial distortion parameters in the block adjustment. Assign a value of 1 for calibration or 0 for no calibration. The default is 0. CalibrateK—Calibrate for tangential distortion parameters in the block adjustment. Assign a value of 1 for calibration or 0 for no calibration. The default is 0. Note: Calibration parameters, such as perspective data, are usually provided for most professional digital aerial cameras, such as UltraCam or DMC. The calibration options can be 0 if camera calibration parameters are prepared in the camera table. APrioriAccuracyX—Include the accuracy of the x-coordinate provided by the airborne Position Orientation System. The units must match PerspectiveX. If the value is set to 0, the x-coordinate of the image location is not adjusted in adjustment. This is not recommended for most UAV data. APrioriAccuracyY—Include the accuracy of the y-coordinate provided by the airborne Position Orientation System. The units must match PerspectiveY. If the value is set to 0, the y-coordinate of the image location is not adjusted in adjustment. This is not recommended for most UAV data. APrioriAccuracyZ—Include the accuracy of the z-coordinate provided by the airborne Position Orientation System. The units must match PerspectiveZ. If the value is set to 0, the z-coordinate of the image location is not adjusted in adjustment. This is not recommended for most UAV data. APrioriAccuracyXY—Include the accuracy of the planar coordinate provided by the metadata. The units must match PerspectiveX. If the value is set to 0, planar coordinates (x and y) of the image location are not adjusted in adjustment. This is not recommended for most UAV data. APrioriAccuracyXYZ—Include the accuracy of image location provided by the metadata. The units must match PerspectiveX. If the value is set to 0, the image location is not adjusted in adjustment. This is not recommended for most UAV data. APrioriAccuracyOmega—Include the accuracy of the Omega angle provided by the airborne Position Orientation System. The units are in decimal degrees. APrioriAccuracyPhi—Include the accuracy of the Phi angle provided by the airborne Position Orientation System. The units are in decimal degrees. APrioriAccuracyOmegaPhi—Include the accuracy of the Omega or Phi angle provided by the airborne Position Orientation System. The units are in decimal degrees. APrioriAccuracyKappa—Include the accuracy of the Kappa angle provided by the airborne Position Orientation System. The units are in decimal degrees. ComputeAntennaOffset—Compute the offset between GNSS antenna center and camera projection center in adjustment. Assign a value of 1 to compute or 0 for no computation. The default is 0. ComputeShift—Compute the GNSS signal shift in flights in bundle adjustment. Assign a value of 1 to compute or 0 for no computation. The default is 0. ComputeImagePosteriorStd—Compute the posterior standard deviation of image location and orientation after adjustment. Assign a value of 1 to compute or 0 for no computation. The default is 1. ComputeSolutionPointPosteriorStd—Compute the posterior standard deviation of solution points after adjustment. Assign a value of 1 to compute or 0 for no computation. The default is 0. rigCamera—Allow processing of a multiple camera rig in the block adjustment. Assign a value of 1 to use the rigCamera module or a value of 0 to not use the rigCamera module. If a value of 1 is assigned, the relationship of multiple cameras in the adjustment will be computed. The default is 0. Value table columns: Name—The name of the adjustment option. Value—The value for the adjustment option."}, {"parameter": "location_accuracy", "label_zh": "影像位置精度 （可選）", "label_en": "Image Location Accuracy (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the geometric accuracy level of the images. This parameter is only active if the Transformation Type parameter is specified as Rational Polynomial Coefficients. If low accuracy is specified, the control points will first be improved by an initial triangulation; then they will be used in the block adjustment calculation. The medium and high accuracy options do not require additional estimation processing. High accuracy—The accuracy will be 30 meters or less. Medium accuracy—The accuracy will be between 31 meters and 100 meters. This is the default. Low accuracy—The accuracy will be more than 100 meters. Very High accuracy—The imagery was collected with a high-accuracy, differential GPS, such as RTK or PPK. This option will keep image locations fixed during block adjustment."}, {"parameter": "out_quality_table", "label_zh": "輸出校正質量表 （可選）", "label_en": "Output Adjustment Quality Table (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Table", "description_en": "An output table used to store adjustment quality information. This parameter is only active if the Transformation Type parameter is specified as Rational Polynomial Coefficients."}, {"parameter": "DEM", "label_zh": "按 DEM 優化 （可選）", "label_en": "Refine by DEM (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Raster Dataset; Raster Layer; Mosaic Dataset; Mosaic Layer", "description_en": "An input DEM from which elevations will be sampled as ground control points for refining the geometric accuracy of the image network in the adjustment. This parameter is only active when the Transformation Type parameter is specified as Frame camera model."}, {"parameter": "elevation_accuracy", "label_zh": "DEM 的高程精度 （可選）", "label_en": "Elevation Accuracy of DEM (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The elevation accuracy of the input DEM. The accuracy value will be used as a weight for the sampled ground control points in the adjustment. This parameter is only active when the Transformation Type parameter is specified as Frame camera model."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.rm.ComputeBlockAdjustment(in_mosaic_dataset, in_control_points, transformation_type, out_solution_table, {out_solution_point_table}, {maximum_residual_value}, {adjustment_options}, {location_accuracy}, {out_quality_table}, {DEM}, {elevation_accuracy})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_mosaic_dataset、in_control_points、transformation_type、out_solution_table、out_solution_point_table、maximum_residual_value、adjustment_options、location_accuracy、out_quality_table、DEM、elevation_accuracy。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 將 CAD 導入室內資料集 (Indoors 工具) · 匯總

**id**　c83d183a4c73ee967a-1

**chapter**

- Indoors 工具箱

**text**　可將圖徵從 CAD 文件導入符合 ArcGIS Indoors 信息模型的室內資料集。 可使用該工具的輸出創建樓層感知型地圖和場景，以及生成用于路由的室內網絡。

**content_version**　3.7

## 將 CAD 導入室內資料集 (Indoors 工具) · 用法

**id**　c83d183a4c73ee967a-2

**english**　Import CAD To Indoor Dataset

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Indoors 工具箱

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html

**text**　可以使用此工具將樓層平面圖資料加載到使用創建室內資料集工具或創建 Indoors 資料庫工具創建的 Indoors 工作空間中。
在運行該工具之前，必須對源 CAD 資料進行地理配準。 工具支持具有空間參考信息的相關 .ebc 文件的資料。
源 CAD 資料參數值必須至少為一個 .dwg 或 .dgn 文件。 注意： 該工具一次導入一個設施點的一個樓層。 如果您有多個 CAD 文件包含同一樓層的資料，則可以將多個 CAD 文件添加到源 CAD 資料參數中。
目標單元圖徵、目標樓層圖徵、目標設施點圖徵和目標細節圖徵參數必須參考符合 Indoors 模型的圖徵類或圖徵圖層。 注: 目標圖徵必須全部來自同一文件地理資料庫、企業級地理資料庫或圖徵服務。 當在目標單元圖徵參數值中添加、更新或刪除圖徵時，該工具會在 ArcGISProTemp 目錄中創建一個匯總文本文件。 匯總文本文件列出了使用 UNIT_ID 和 NAME 欄位的所有已添加、更新或刪除的單元圖徵。
針對樓層名稱參數，提供樓層的名稱。 如果該樓層存在于目標 Levels 圖層中，則可以從下拉菜單中進行選擇。 如果選擇現有 Levels 圖層，則將更新 Levels 圖徵以及關聯的 Units 和 Details 圖徵。 如果未使用 CAD 注記映射參數進行映射，則將保留默認情況下未填充的 Levels 和 Units 圖層中欄位的欄位值。 將刪除并覆蓋細節圖徵。
可以從輸入 CAD 資料 CAD 圖層導入圖徵，或者將 CAD 資料添加至地圖并從折線圖層導入圖徵。 可以使用從源 CAD 選擇圖層參數以指定是從輸入 CAD 圖層還是從地圖折線圖層導入。 如果選中從源 CAD 選擇圖層參數，則需要為 Units 的源 CAD 圖層、Levels 的源 CAD 圖層、Details 的源 CAD 圖層和 Facilities 的源 CAD 圖層參數提供 CAD 圖層。 或者，在 Doors 的源 CAD 圖層參數中包含門實體。 如果取消選中從源 CAD 選擇圖層參數，則需要為 Units 的源圖層、Levels 的源圖層、Details 的源圖層和 Facilities 的源圖層參數提供 CAD 折線地圖圖層。 或者，在 Doors 的源圖層參數中包含門實體。 該工具支持在輸入地圖圖層進行選擇和定義查詢。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html#usage

## 將 CAD 導入室內資料集 (Indoors 工具) · 用法

**id**　c83d183a4c73ee967a-3

**english**　Import CAD To Indoor Dataset

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Indoors 工具箱

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html

**text**　選中從源 CAD 選擇圖層參數時，該工具會創建一個配置文件。 配置文件存儲了折線幾何和注記信息的所有 CAD 映射以及高級選項設置。 您可以將此文件用作后續工具運行中具有相同或相似 CAD 圖層和映射的其他 CAD 文件的樓層平面圖配置文件參數的輸入。 這有助于確保多次導入之間的一致性和效率。 選中從源 CAD 選擇圖層參數時，樓層平面圖配置文件參數可用。 使用此參數導入 JSON 配置文件，快速設置該工具，以方便具有相同或相似 CAD 圖層和映射的其他 CAD 文件的后續運行。 在以下情況下，該工具不會創建配置文件： 取消選中從源 CAD 選擇圖層參數。 您提供樓層平面圖配置文件參數值，并且不再對參數設置進行任何更改。 如果選中從源 CAD 選擇圖層參數，則會創建配置文件。 如果您為樓層平面圖配置文件參數指定了配置文件，并且沒有對參數設置進行進一步更改，則不會創建配置文件。 在為樓層平面圖配置文件參數提供配置文件時，可以對參數進行進一步的調整。 例如，如果運行該工具的 CAD 文件包含第一個 CAD 文件中不存在的附加 CAD 圖層，則可以提供平面圖配置文件，然后將附加的圖層添加到 CAD 映射參數中。 如果導入配置后對參數設置進行了更改，則將創建一個新的輸出配置文件。 輸出配置文件的默認輸出位置是 ArcGIS Pro 工程的主文件夾，或者如果工具在工程外運行則為臨時文件夾。 配置文件可能包含以下參數的值： Units 的源 CAD 圖層 Levels 的源 CAD 圖層 Doors 的源 CAD 圖層 Details 的源 CAD 圖層 Facilities 的源 CAD 圖層 CAD 注記映射 目標室內圖層 目標欄位 類型 源 CAD 圖層 塊屬性 分隔符 位置 高級選項 門到墻壁的距離 最小單元寬度 最小單元面積 注: 如果在歷史記錄窗格中打開了以前的工具運行，該工具將顯示從配置文件加載的參數。 加載配置文件后對參數所做的更改只有在右鍵單擊并選擇上次工具運行的查看詳細信息時才可見。
為 Units 的源 CAD 圖層或 Units 的源圖層參數提供表示單元邊界的折線圖層。 可能包括代表房間邊界的單個圖層，或者經過組合后表示房間邊界的多個圖層（例如外墻、內墻和門的圖層）。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html#usage

## 將 CAD 導入室內資料集 (Indoors 工具) · 用法

**id**　c83d183a4c73ee967a-4

**english**　Import CAD To Indoor Dataset

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Indoors 工具箱

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html

**text**　為 Levels 的源 CAD 圖層或 Levels 的源圖層參數提供表示級別邊界的折線圖層。 如果沒有為任一參數提供值，則該工具將根據單元面的范圍創建級別面圖徵。
將樓層高程參數值設置為相對于地面的樓層高程。 該參數值必須包含數值和測量單位（國際英尺或米）。 將基于此值填充 Levels、Units 和 Details 的 Z 值。
為垂直順序參數值提供表示每個樓層的垂直順序的序數整數。 一樓的垂直順序為零 (0)。 對于一樓之上的樓層，垂直順序值為正；對于一樓之下的樓層，則為負。
為 Facilites 的源 CAD 圖層或 Facilites 的源圖層參數提供表示設施點邊界的折線圖層。 如果沒有為任一參數提供值，則該工具將根據設施點中所有樓層面的范圍創建或更新設施點面。
為 Details 的源 CAD 圖層或 Details 的源圖層參數提供表示要在細節圖層（例如墻壁、窗戶、門、家具等）中作為折線圖徵導入的圖徵的折線圖層。
如果輸入 CAD 資料包含必須閉合才能創建單元、樓層或設施點邊界的門圖徵，請使用以下參數以閉合門： 對于 Doors 的源 CAD 圖層或 Doors 的源圖層參數，請提供包含門圖徵的 CAD 或地圖圖層。 或者，提供門到墻壁的距離參數的值。 該參數值用于指示工具從門圖徵搜索相關單元的邊界的距離。 該參數值必須包含數值和測量單位（國際英寸或毫米）。 默認值為 0.3 國際英寸。
可以使用 CAD 注記映射參數將 CAD 注記映射到 Indoors 模型中的圖徵。 建議您至少將 CAD 中的信息映射到 Units 圖層中的 NAME 和 USE_TYPE 欄位。 這些欄位用于支持 ArcGIS Pro 和 Indoors web 及移動應用程序中的功能。 當重新運行工具以更新 Indoors 工作空間中的圖徵時，NAME 欄位也可用于更新方案。 該參數支持根據塊或文本映射注記，以及映射閉合折線實體的實體句柄。 句柄是分配給 .dwg 文件中實體的唯一標識符。 您可以將句柄信息映射到 Units、Facilities 或 Levels 圖層中的文本類型欄位。 當輸入 CAD 文件中的單個閉合折線實體表示 Indoors 工作空間中的單個面時，支持映射句柄。 將 CAD 注記映射的值設置為如下所示： 目標室內圖層—指定 Indoors 工作空間中要將注記映射到的圖層。 您可以將 Units、Levels 或 Facilities 圖層設置為此值的輸入，它們對應于為目標單元圖徵、目標樓層圖徵和目標設施點圖徵參數提供的值。 不支持將注記映射到 Details 圖層。 目標欄位—提供要將 CAD 注記映射到的目標室內圖層中的欄位。 類型—指定正在映射的 CAD 注記的實體類型（文本、塊或句柄）。 如果源 CAD 資料中的 CAD 注記為 Text 或 MText，請選擇“文本”。 如果 CAD 注記存儲為塊注記，請選擇“塊”。 要將實體句柄映射到 Indoors 工作空間，請選擇“句柄”。 源 CAD 圖層—提供包含要映射的 CAD 注記的 CAD 圖層。 塊屬性—如果將類型值設置為“塊”，請提供存儲要映射到目標欄位的 CAD 注記的塊屬性的名稱。 如果將類型值設置為“文本”或“句柄”，請勿提供值。 分隔符—如果要映射到目標欄位的值存儲為分隔文本，則需要為此值設置分隔符。 例如，如果 CAD 注記以句點分隔，例如 CAMPUS.A.A-210，并且您希望將字符串 (A-210) 的第三部分映射為 UNIT_NAME 欄位值，請使用句點 (.) 作為分隔符值。 位置—如果要映射到目標欄位的值存儲為分隔文本，則需要設置要映射的值的位置，其中字符串的開頭為位置 1。 例如，如果 CAD 注記以句點分隔，例如 CAMPUS.A.A-210，并且您希望將字符串 (A-210) 的第三部分映射為 UNIT_NAME 欄位值，請使用 3 作為位置值。 注: 當針對 Units 圖徵類時，如果 CAD 注記源于多個 CAD 圖層，則可以向針對同一欄位的 CAD 注記映射參數提供多個輸入。 如果多個指定 CAD 圖層包含 Units 圖徵的注記，則該工具將使用提供的第一個包含該圖徵的注記的 CAD 圖層中的 CAD 注記。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html#usage

## 將 CAD 導入室內資料集 (Indoors 工具) · 用法

**id**　c83d183a4c73ee967a-5

**english**　Import CAD To Indoor Dataset

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Indoors 工具箱

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html

**text**　最小單元寬度參數用于指示面必須被視為 Units 圖徵的最小寬度。 該參數值必須包含數值和測量單位（國際英尺或米）。 默認值為 3 國際英尺。
最小單元面積參數用于指示面必須被視為單元圖徵的最小面積。 該參數值必須包含數值和測量單位（國際平方英尺或平方米）。 默認值為 9 國際平方英尺。
對于任何不滿足由最小單元寬度或最小單元面積參數設置的閾值的面，會將其復制到 ArcGIS Pro 工程默認地理資料庫中的臨時圖徵類。 可以檢查臨時圖徵類面并根據需要將其中任何一個追加到 Units 圖層。
如果存在與樓層名稱參數值同名的 Levels 圖徵，則將按如下方式更新該 Levels 圖徵及其關聯的 Units 和 Details 圖徵： 將更新 Levels 圖徵。 將保留默認情況下未填充的任何屬性，包括自定義欄位。 該工具將比較 CAD 工程圖和 Units 圖層之間的 Units 圖徵。 如果找到具有匹配名稱或幾何的單元，則將對其進行更新。 將保留該工具默認情況下未填充且未使用 CAD 注記映射參數進行映射的任何欄位。 將添加 CAD 工程圖中存在，但 Units 圖層中不存在的圖徵。 將刪除 CAD 工程圖中不存在，但 Units 圖層中存在的圖徵。 將從 CAD 工程圖中刪除 Details 圖徵并將其重新導入。 如果為單元提供了地圖圖層作為輸入，則該工具將遵循應用于這些圖層的所有定義查詢或選擇。 注: 如果樓層名稱參數值與現有 Levels 圖徵的 NAME 欄位值不匹配，則該工具將導入 CAD 工程圖作為具有指定樓層名稱值的新樓層。
間隙容差參數可用于設置輸入圖徵懸空端點的鄰近捕捉容差。 這有助于減少不完整單元或未使用的線段的數量。 默認值為 0.039 國際英寸（1 毫米）。 注: 增加間隙容差會暫時增加工具處理過程中的 x,y 容差，這可能會導致導入幾何圖形時的精度降低。 增加間隙容差參數值時，運行該工具后驗證導入幾何體的準確性。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html#usage

## 將 CAD 導入室內資料集 (Indoors 工具) · 參數

**id**　c83d183a4c73ee967a-27

**english**　Import CAD To Indoor Dataset

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Indoors 工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html

**parameter**　cad_annotation_mapping

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- CAD 注記映射 （可選）
- 指定 CAD 注記圖徵的欄位映射以填充 Indoors 工作空間中圖層的欄位。 值表列： Target Indoor Layer—The layer in the Indoors workspace where the annotation will be mapped. The tool supports mapping to the layer provided for the Target Facilities, Target Levels, and Target Units parameters. Facilities—The target Facilities layer where the annotation will be mapped. Levels—The target Levels layer where the annotation will be mapped. Units—The target Units layer where the annotation will be mapped. Target Field—The field in the target layer where the annotation will be mapped. The field must already exist. Type—The type of annotation that will be mapped. Block—Text annotation will be mapped. Text—Block annotation will be mapped. Handle—Handle annotation will be mapped. Source CAD Layer—The CAD layer that contains the annotation that will be mapped. Block Attribute—The block attribute that contains the information to map for annotation that has the Block type. Delimiter—The delimiting character that will be used for annotation stored in a delimited string. Position—The position of the value that will be mapped for annotation stored in a delimited string.
- Value Table

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html#parameters

**id**　c83d183a4c73ee967a-44

**english**　Import CAD To Indoor Dataset

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Indoors 工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html

**parameter**　allow_layers_from_cad

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- allow_layers_from_cad （可選）
- 指定表示單元邊界的折線是源自 CAD 文件，還是源自地圖圖徵圖層。 如果指定從地圖圖徵圖層獲取源，則可以在圖層上進行選擇以導入圖徵子集。 ALLOW_LAYERS_FROM_CAD—表示單元邊界的折線將直接源自 CAD 文件。 這是默認設置。 NO_ALLOW_LAYERS_FROM_CAD—表示單元邊界的折線將源自地圖圖徵圖層。
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html#derived-output

## 將 CAD 導入室內資料集 (Indoors 工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　c83d183a4c73ee967a-55

**english**　Import CAD To Indoor Dataset

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Indoors 工具箱

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html

**parameter**　cad_annotation_mapping

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- cad_annotation_mapping [cad_annotation_mapping,...] （可選）
- 指定 CAD 注記圖徵的欄位映射以填充 Indoors 工作空間中圖層的欄位。 值表列： Target Indoor Layer—The layer in the Indoors workspace where the annotation will be mapped. The tool supports mapping to the layer provided for the Target Facilities, Target Levels, and Target Units parameters. Facilities—The target Facilities layer where the annotation will be mapped. Levels—The target Levels layer where the annotation will be mapped. Units—The target Units layer where the annotation will be mapped. Target Field—The field in the target layer where the annotation will be mapped. The field must already exist. Type—The type of annotation that will be mapped. Block—Text annotation will be mapped. Text—Block annotation will be mapped. Handle—Handle annotation will be mapped. Source CAD Layer—The CAD layer that contains the annotation that will be mapped. Block Attribute—The block attribute that contains the information to map for annotation that has the Block type. Delimiter—The delimiting character that will be used for annotation stored in a delimited string. Position—The position of the value that will be mapped for annotation stored in a delimited string.
- Value Table

**text**　ImportCADToIndoorDataset 示例 3（獨立腳本）
以下獨立腳本演示了如何使用 ImportCADToIndoorDataset 函數。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html#derived-output

## 將 CAD 導入室內資料集 (Indoors 工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　c83d183a4c73ee967a-67

**english**　Import CAD To Indoor Dataset

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Indoors 工具箱

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html

**text**　import arcpy

#Set the CAD dataset to import
input_cad_datasets = 'C:/Indoors/Building_H/Bldg_H_2.dwg'

#Set target layers that conform to the ArcGIS Indoors Information model
target_unit_features = 'C:/Indoors/ExampleCampus.gdb/Indoor/Units'
target_detail_features = 'C:/Indoors/ExampleCampus.gdb/Indoor/Details'
target_level_features = 'C:/Indoors/ExampleCampus.gdb/Indoor/Levels'
target_facility_features = 'C:/Indoors/ExampleCampus.gdb/Indoor/Facilities'

#Set the level information
level_name = "H2"
vertical_order = 1
level_information = "8 FeetInt"

#Set the facility name
facility_name = "Building H"

# Choose to import CAD layers added to a map,
# create the map layers from polyline features in CAD
allow_layers_from_cad = "NO_ALLOW_LAYERS_FROM_CAD"
input_unit_layers_cad = ''
in_CAD_fc = "C:/Indoors/Building_H/Bldg_H_2.dwg/Polyline"
input_unit_feature_layers = arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(in_CAD_fc, 'units_feature_layer', "Layer IN('A-DOOR', 'A-WALL', 'I-WALL')")
input_level_layers_cad = ''
input_level_feature_layers = arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(in_CAD_fc, 'level_feature_layer', "Layer = 'A-FLOOR-OUTLINE'")
input_facility_layers_cad = ''
input_facility_feature_layers = arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(in_CAD_fc, 'facility_feature_layer', "Layer = 'A-BLDG-OUTLINE'")
input_door_layers_cad = ''
input_door_feature_layers = arcpy.management.MakeFeatureLayer(in_CAD_fc, 'level_feature_layer', "Layer = 'A-DOOR'")

#Map CAD annotation
cad_annotation_mapping = 'Units NAME Text A-AREA-IDEN # # #;'\
                         'Units USE_TYPE Block A-ROOM-INFO ROOMUSE # #;'\
                         'Facilities NAME_LONG Text A-BLDG-INFO # . 2'

# Set the minimum width and area that should be considered a unit feature
door_close_buffer = ''
input_unit_minimum_width = ""
input_unit_minimum_area = ""

# Do not use settings stored in a configuration file
floor_plan_config_file = ""

#Set gap tolerance
input_gap_tolerance="0.039 InchesInt"

# Run the tool
arcpy.indoors.ImportCADToIndoorDataset(input_cad_datasets, target_level_features, level_name, vertical_order,
                                       level_elevation, target_facility_features, facility_name, target_unit_features,
                                       target_detail_features, allow_layers_from_cad,
                                       input_unit_layers_cad, input_unit_feature_layers,
                                       input_level_layers_cad, input_level_feature_layers,
                                       input_door_layers_cad, input_door_feature_layers,
                                       input_facility_layers_cad, input_facility_feature_layers,
                                       cad_annotation_mapping, door_close_buffer,
                                       input_unit_minimum_width, input_unit_minimum_area, floor_plan_config_file, input_gap_tolerance)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html

**text**　import arcpy

#Set the CAD dataset to import
input_cad_datasets = 'C:/Indoors/Building_H/Bldg_H_2.dwg'

#Set target layers that conform to the ArcGIS Indoors Information model
target_unit_features = 'C:/Indoors/ExampleCampus.gdb/Indoor/Units'
target_detail_features = 'C:/Indoors/ExampleCampus.gdb/Indoor/Details'
target_level_features = 'C:/Indoors/ExampleCampus.gdb/Indoor/Levels'
target_facility_features = 'C:/Indoors/ExampleCampus.gdb/Indoor/Facilities'

#Set the level information
level_name = "H2"
vertical_order = 1
level_information = "8 FeetInt"

#Set the facility name
facility_name = "Building H"

# Choose to import CAD layers as specified in a configuration file
allow_layers_from_cad = "ALLOW_LAYERS_FROM_CAD"
input_unit_layers_cad = ""
input_unit_feature_layers = ""
input_level_layers_cad = ""
input_level_feature_layers = ""
input_facility_layers_cad = ""
input_facility_feature_layers = ""
input_door_layers_cad = ""
input_door_feature_layers = ""

#Map CAD annotation as specified in a configuration file
cad_annotation_mapping = ""

# Set advanced options as specified in a configuration file
door_close_buffer = ""
input_unit_minimum_width = ""
input_unit_minimum_area = ""

# Use settings stored in this configuration file
floor_plan_config_file = "C:/Indoors/Bldg_H_1_06_10_2024_16_13_19.json"

# Set gap tolerance
input_gap_tolerance="0.039 InchesInt"

# Run the tool
arcpy.indoors.ImportCADToIndoorDataset(input_cad_datasets, target_level_features, level_name, vertical_order,
                                       level_elevation, target_facility_features, facility_name, target_unit_features,
                                       target_detail_features, allow_layers_from_cad,
                                       input_unit_layers_cad, input_unit_feature_layers,
                                       input_level_layers_cad, input_level_feature_layers,
                                       input_door_layers_cad, input_door_feature_layers,
                                       input_facility_layers_cad, input_facility_feature_layers,
                                       cad_annotation_mapping, door_close_buffer,
                                       input_unit_minimum_width, input_unit_minimum_area, floor_plan_config_file, input_gap_tolerance)

## 將 CAD 導入室內資料集 (Indoors 工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- Indoors 工具箱

**english**　Import CAD To Indoor Dataset

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html

**id**　c83d183a4c73ee967a-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Import CAD To Indoor Dataset，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。源 CAD 資料 [input_cad_datasets]、目標樓層圖徵 [target_level_features]、樓層名稱 [level_name]、垂直順序 [vertical_order]、樓層的高程 [level_elevation]、目標設施點圖徵 [target_facility_features]、設施點名稱 [facility_name]、目標單元圖徵 [target_unit_features]、目標細節圖徵 [target_detail_features]、從源 CAD 選擇圖層 （可選） [allow_layers_from_cad]、Units 的源 CAD 圖層 （可選） [input_unit_layers_cad]、Units 的源圖層 （可選） [input_unit_feature_layers]、Levels 的源 CAD 圖層 （可選） [input_level_layers_cad]、Levels 的源圖層 （可選） [input_level_feature_layers]、Doors 的源 CAD 圖層 （可選） [input_door_layers_cad]、Doors 的源圖層 （可選） [input_door_feature_layers]、Details 的源 CAD 圖層 （可選） [input_detail_layers_cad]、Details 的源圖層 （可選） [input_detail_feature_layers]、Facilities 的源 CAD 圖層 （可選） [input_facility_layers_cad]、Facilities 的源圖層 （可選） [input_facility_feature_layers]、CAD 注記映射 （可選） [cad_annotation_mapping]、門到墻壁的距離 （可選） [door_close_buffer]、最小單元寬度 （可選） [input_unit_minimum_width]、最小單元面積 （可選） [input_unit_minimum_area]、樓層平面圖配置文件 （可選） [floor_plan_config_file]、間隙容差 （可選） [input_gap_tolerance]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/indoors/import-cad-to-indoor-dataset.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "input_cad_datasets", "label_zh": "源 CAD 資料", "label_en": "Source CAD Data", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "CAD Drawing Dataset", "description_en": "The .dwg or .dgn files that contain floor plan information that will be imported to the Indoors model."}, {"parameter": "target_level_features", "label_zh": "目標樓層圖徵", "label_en": "Target Level Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The target Levels feature layer, feature class, or feature service that conforms to the Indoors model and resides in the same workspace as the target Facilities, Units, and Details features."}, {"parameter": "level_name", "label_zh": "樓層名稱", "label_en": "Level Name", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The unique level name of the level on which the source CAD data is located."}, {"parameter": "vertical_order", "label_zh": "垂直順序", "label_en": "Vertical Order", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Long", "description_en": "An ordinal integer representing the vertical order of each floor. The vertical order of the ground floor is zero (0). Floors above the ground floor have positive vertical order values, and floors below the ground floor have negative values."}, {"parameter": "level_elevation", "label_zh": "樓層的高程", "label_en": "Elevation Of Level", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The elevation of the level relative to a flat terrain. This value will be used to populate the z-value for Levels, Units, and Details features."}, {"parameter": "target_facility_features", "label_zh": "目標設施點圖徵", "label_en": "Target Facility Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The target Facilities feature layer, feature class, or feature service that conforms to the Indoors model and resides in the same workspace as the target Levels, Units, and Details features."}, {"parameter": "facility_name", "label_zh": "設施點名稱", "label_en": "Facility Name", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The unique facility name of the building where the source CAD data is located."}, {"parameter": "target_unit_features", "label_zh": "目標單元圖徵", "label_en": "Target Unit Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The target Units feature layer, feature class, or feature service that conforms to the Indoors model and resides in the same workspace as the target Facilities, Levels, and Details features."}, {"parameter": "target_detail_features", "label_zh": "目標細節圖徵", "label_en": "Target Detail Features", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The target Details feature layer, feature class, or feature service that conforms to the Indoors model and resides in the same workspace as the target Facilities, Levels, and Units features."}, {"parameter": "allow_layers_from_cad", "label_zh": "從源 CAD 選擇圖層 （可選）", "label_en": "Select Layer From Source CAD (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether polylines that represent unit boundaries will be sourced from CAD files or from map feature layers. If you specify to source from map feature layers, you can make a selection on the layer to import a subset of features. Checked—Polylines that represent unit boundaries will be sourced directly from the CAD files. This is the default. Unchecked—Polylines that represent unit boundaries will be sourced from map feature layers."}, {"parameter": "input_unit_layers_cad", "label_zh": "Units 的源 CAD 圖層 （可選）", "label_en": "Source CAD Layers For Units (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The CAD layers that contain polyline entities that define the edges and extent of the usable spaces within a facility. These polylines will be used to create unit polygon features in the target Units layer."}, {"parameter": "input_unit_feature_layers", "label_zh": "Units 的源圖層 （可選）", "label_en": "Source Layers For Units (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The feature layers that contain polyline entities that define the edges and extent of the usable spaces within a facility. These polylines will be used to create unit polygon features in the target Units layer."}, {"parameter": "input_level_layers_cad", "label_zh": "Levels 的源 CAD 圖層 （可選）", "label_en": "Source CAD Layers For Levels (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The CAD layers that contain polyline entities that define the edges and extent of the level. These polylines will be used to create unit polygon features in the target Levels layer."}, {"parameter": "input_level_feature_layers", "label_zh": "Levels 的源圖層 （可選）", "label_en": "Source Layers For Levels (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The feature layers that contain polyline entities that define the edges and extent of the level. These polylines will be used to create unit polygon features in the target Levels layer."}, {"parameter": "input_door_layers_cad", "label_zh": "Doors 的源 CAD 圖層 （可選）", "label_en": "Source CAD Layers For Doors (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The CAD layers that contain polyline entities that define the doors that are part of a unit boundary. These polylines will be closed to create unit polygon features in the target Units layer."}, {"parameter": "input_door_feature_layers", "label_zh": "Doors 的源圖層 （可選）", "label_en": "Source Layers For Doors (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The feature layers that contain polyline entities that define the doors that are part of a unit boundary. These polylines will be closed to create unit polygon features in the target Units layer."}, {"parameter": "input_detail_layers_cad", "label_zh": "Details 的源 CAD 圖層 （可選）", "label_en": "Source CAD Layers For Details (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The CAD layers that contain polyline entities that represent floor-plan details—such as walls, windows, and doors—that will be included as polyline features in the target Details layer."}, {"parameter": "input_detail_feature_layers", "label_zh": "Details 的源圖層 （可選）", "label_en": "Source Layers For Details (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The feature layers that contain polyline entities that represent floor-plan details—such as walls, windows, and doors—that will be included as polyline features in the target Details layer."}, {"parameter": "input_facility_layers_cad", "label_zh": "Facilities 的源 CAD 圖層 （可選）", "label_en": "Source CAD Layers For Facilities (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The CAD layers that contain polyline entities that define the edges and extent of the facility footprint. If no value is provided, the facility footprint will be created or updated based on the extent of all levels within the facility."}, {"parameter": "input_facility_feature_layers", "label_zh": "Facilities 的源圖層 （可選）", "label_en": "Source Layers For Facilities (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The feature layers that contain polyline entities that define the edges and extent of the facility footprint. If no value is provided, the facility footprint will be created or updated based on the extent of all levels within the facility."}, {"parameter": "cad_annotation_mapping", "label_zh": "CAD 注記映射 （可選）", "label_en": "CAD Annotation Mapping (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Value Table", "description_en": "Specifies the field mapping for CAD annotation features to populate a field of a layer in the Indoors workspace. Value table columns: Target Indoor Layer—The layer in the Indoors workspace where the annotation will be mapped. The tool supports mapping to the layer provided for the Target Facilities, Target Levels, and Target Units parameters. Facilities—The target Facilities layer where the annotation will be mapped. Levels—The target Levels layer where the annotation will be mapped. Units—The target Units layer where the annotation will be mapped. Target Field—The field in the target layer where the annotation will be mapped. The field must already exist. Type—The type of annotation that will be mapped. Block—Text annotation will be mapped. Text—Block annotation will be mapped. Handle—Handle annotation will be mapped. Source CAD Layer—The CAD layer that contains the annotation that will be mapped. Block Attribute—The block attribute that contains the information to map for annotation that has the Block type. Delimiter—The delimiting character that will be used for annotation stored in a delimited string. Position—The position of the value that will be mapped for annotation stored in a delimited string."}, {"parameter": "door_close_buffer", "label_zh": "門到墻壁的距離 （可選）", "label_en": "Door To Wall Distance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The distance the tool will search from a door feature for a unit boundary in international inches or millimeters. The default is 0.3 international inches."}, {"parameter": "input_unit_minimum_width", "label_zh": "最小單元寬度 （可選）", "label_en": "Minimum Unit Width (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The minimum width in international feet or meters that a space must be to be considered a unit feature. Features with a width below this threshold will be written to a nonunit polygons feature class and will not be included in the target Units layer. The default is 3 international feet."}, {"parameter": "input_unit_minimum_area", "label_zh": "最小單元面積 （可選）", "label_en": "Minimum Unit Area (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Areal Unit", "description_en": "The minimum area in square international feet or square meters that a space must be to be considered a unit feature. Features with an area below this threshold will be written to a nonunit polygons feature class and will not be included in the target Units layer. The default is 9 square international feet."}, {"parameter": "floor_plan_config_file", "label_zh": "樓層平面圖配置文件 （可選）", "label_en": "Floor Plan Configuration File (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "File", "description_en": "A JSON configuration file containing preset parameter values. Provide the configuration file from a previous tool run to populate parameters in the tool for CAD files with the same or similar CAD layers and mappings. When a configuration file is provided, changes can be made to the other parameter values before running the tool. If other parameter changes are made, a new output configuration file will be created. This parameter is only available when the Select Layer From Source CAD parameter is checked."}, {"parameter": "input_gap_tolerance", "label_zh": "間隙容差 （可選）", "label_en": "Gap Tolerance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The proximity tolerance for dangling endpoints of input features that will be snapped together when generating units. Values can be provided in millimeters or international inches. The default is 0.039 international inches (1 millimeter)."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.indoors.ImportCADToIndoorDataset(input_cad_datasets, target_level_features, level_name, vertical_order, level_elevation, target_facility_features, facility_name, target_unit_features, target_detail_features, {allow_layers_from_cad}, {input_unit_layers_cad}, {input_unit_feature_layers}, {input_level_layers_cad}, {input_level_feature_layers}, {input_door_layers_cad}, {input_door_feature_layers}, {input_detail_layers_cad}, {input_detail_feature_layers}, {input_facility_layers_cad}, {input_facility_feature_layers}, {cad_annotation_mapping}, {door_close_buffer}, {input_unit_minimum_width}, {input_unit_minimum_area}, {floor_plan_config_file}, {input_gap_tolerance})
